+       1617: // Custom Wave Painter for Connection Animation
+       1618: class WavePainter extends CustomPainter {
+       1619:   final double progress;
+       1620:   final Color color1;
+       1621:   final Color color2;
+       1622: 
+       1623:   WavePainter({
+       1624:     required this.progress,
+       1625:     required this.color1,
+       1626:     required this.color2,
+       1627:   });
+       1628: 
+       1629:   @override
+       1630:   void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
+       1631:     final paint = Paint()
+       1632:       ..shader = LinearGradient(
+       1633:         colors: [color1, color2],
+       1634:       ).createShader(Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, size.width, size.height))
+       1635:       ..strokeWidth = 2
+       1636:       ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke;
+       1637: 
+       1638:     final path = Path();
+       1639:     path.moveTo(0, size.height / 2);
+       1640: 
+       1641:     for (double x = 0; x <= size.width; x++) {
+       1642:       final y = size.height / 2 +
+       1643:           10 * math.sin((x / size.width * 4 * math.pi) + (progress * 2 * math.pi));
+       1644:       path.lineTo(x, y);
+       1645:     }
+       1646: 
+       1647:     canvas.drawPath(path, paint);
+       1648:   }
+       1649: 
+       1650:   @override
+       1651:   bool shouldRepaint(WavePainter oldDelegate) => true;
+       1652: }

## OBJECTIVE
Redesign the Flutter app's home screen (anasayfa) based on the design system in `/home/mrt/test/anonim/ds/` folder, implementing a luminous cosmic aesthetic with proper animations, glows, and responsive layout.

## USER GUIDANCE
- Follow the design in `ds/` folder closely (includes DESIGN.md, screen.png, code.html)
- Apply design system colors, typography (Sora + Manrope), spacing, and shapes throughout
- Avoid excessive red tones - user rejected the initial red/burgundy (#B9003F) as "kırmızı boğukluğu"
- Add impressive animations and glows - user expects world-class design with effects
- Make buttons visually striking, not plain/boring
- Ensure all elements fit on screen properly (responsive)
- Don't create a "rainbow" effect with too many colors
- Incorporate elements from existing theme while applying new design system

   - DESIGN.md: Luminous Cosmic Social aesthetic, Material 3-inspired tokens

   - Typography: Sora (headlines, display), Manrope (body, labels)

    61,   61: 
    62,   62:   Future<void> _checkForMandatoryUpdate() async {
    63,   63:     await Future.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 1));
    64,   64:     if (!mounted) return;
    65,   65:     
    66,   66:     try {
    67,   67:       final updateInfo = await _updateService.checkForUpdate();
    68,   68:       if (updateInfo != null && mounted) {
    69,   69:         if (updateInfo['isMandatory'] == true) {
    70,   70:           _showMandatoryUpdateDialog(updateInfo);
    71,   71:         } else {
    72,   72:           _showUpdateAvailableSnackbar();
    73,   73:         }
    74,   74:       }
    75,   75:     } catch (e) {
    76,   76:       debugPrint('[HOME] Güncelleme kontrolü hatası: $e');
    77,   77:     }
    78,   78:   }
    79,   79: 
    80,   80:   void _showMandatoryUpdateDialog(Map<String, dynamic> updateInfo) {
    81,   81:     showDialog(
    82,   82:       context: context,
    83,   83:       barrierDismissible: false,
    84,   84:       builder: (context) => MandatoryUpdateDialog(updateInfo: updateInfo),
    85,   85:     );
    86,   86:   }
    87,   87: 
    88,   88:   Future<void> _checkForUpdates() async {
    89,   89:     if (_isCheckingUpdate) return;
    90,   90:     setState(() => _isCheckingUpdate = true);
    91,   91:     
    92,   92:     try {
    93,   93:       final updateInfo = await _updateService.checkForUpdate(forceCheck: true);
    94,   94:       if (!mounted) return;
    95,   95: 
    96,   96:       if (updateInfo != null) {
    97,   97:         if (updateInfo['isMandatory'] == true) {
    98,   98:           _showMandatoryUpdateDialog(updateInfo);
    99,   99:         } else {
   100,  100:           showDialog(
   101,  101:             context: context,
   102,  102:             barrierDismissible: true,
   103,  103:             builder: (context) => UpdateDialog(updateInfo: updateInfo),
   104,  104:           );
   105,  105:         }
   106,  106:       } else {
   107,  107:         _showMessage('Uygulamanız güncel');
   108,  108:       }
   109,  109:     } catch (e) {
   110,  110:       if (mounted) {
   111,  111:         _showMessage('Güncelleme kontrolü başarısız', isError: true);
   112,  112:       }
   113,  113:     } finally {
   114,  114:       if (mounted) {
   115,  115:         setState(() => _isCheckingUpdate = false);
   116,  116:       }
   117,  117:     }
   118,  118:   }

  1653, 1653: 
  1654, 1654: // Custom Wave Painter for Connection Animation
  1655, 1655: class WavePainter extends CustomPainter {
  1656, 1656:   final double progress;
  1657, 1657:   final Color color1;
  1658, 1658:   final Color color2;
  1659, 1659: 
  1660, 1660:   WavePainter({
  1661, 1661:     required this.progress,
  1662, 1662:     required this.color1,
  1663, 1663:     required this.color2,
  1664, 1664:   });
  1665, 1665: 
  1666, 1666:   @override
  1667, 1667:   void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
  1668, 1668:     final paint = Paint()
  1669, 1669:       ..shader = LinearGradient(
  1670, 1670:         colors: [color1, color2],
  1671, 1671:       ).createShader(Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, size.width, size.height))
  1672, 1672:       ..strokeWidth = 2.5
  1673, 1673:       ..strokeCap = StrokeCap.round
  1674, 1674:       ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke;
  1675, 1675: 
  1676, 1676:     final path = Path();
  1677, 1677:     path.moveTo(0, size.height / 2);
  1678, 1678: 
  1679, 1679:     for (double x = 0; x <= size.width; x++) {
  1680, 1680:       final y = size.height / 2 +
  1681, 1681:           12 * math.sin((x / size.width * 4 * math.pi) + (progress * 2 * math.pi));
  1682, 1682:       path.lineTo(x, y);
  1683, 1683:     }
  1684, 1684: 
  1685, 1685:     canvas.drawPath(path, paint);
  1686, 1686:   }
  1687, 1687: 
  1688, 1688:   @override
  1689, 1689:   bool shouldRepaint(WavePainter oldDelegate) => true;
  1690, 1690: }

- **Pill-shaped buttons** (9999px border-radius)
- **Frosted glass** efektler
- **Ambient glow** shadow'lar (neon halo efekti)

- Pulse, glow, float, rotate animasyonları korundu
- Renk geçişleri daha canlı

- Metinler: Manrope Regular/Medium
- Letter-spacing ve line-height optimizasyonu

> Perfect! Hiç hata yok. Şimdi özet:

## ✨ TAMAM! PEMBE GİTTİ - TEMİZ TASARIM ✨

### 🎨 Yapılan Değişiklikler:

#### **1. Renk Paleti - Sadece Coral & Cyan**
- ❌ Kaldırıldı: Tüm pembe tonlar (primaryLight), amber vurgular
- ✅ Sadece: Neon Coral (#FF2E63) ↔ Electric Cyan (#00C4CC)
- Tüm gradientler artık Coral→Cyan veya Cyan→Coral
- Amber vurgular cyan ile değiştirildi

#### **2. Butonlar - Minimal ve Temiz**
- **Primary CTA:** 28px border-radius (pill değil), Coral→Cyan gradient
- **Secondary:** Beyaz arka plan, ince border, soft glow
- **Small buttons:** 18px radius, border ekli, daha minimal
- Tüm glow efektleri %50 azaltıldı (0.12-0.25 opacity)

#### **3. Kartlar - Daha Soft**
- Border radius: 32px → 28px (daha az aggressive)
- Shadow'lar daha soft (blurRadius 12-20px)
- Tüm pill-shaped (9999px) elementler → yuvarlatılmış ama pill olmayan (18-28px)
- Voice orb'lar: gradient kaldırıldı, solid beyaz arka plan

#### **4. Badge & Indicator'lar**
- Hepsi artık cyan veya Coral→Cyan gradient
- Amber tonu tamamen kaldırıldı
- "CANLI" badge'i: 12px radius (pill değil)
- Stats badge'leri: border ekli, daha soft

#### **5. Floating Nav Bar**
- 28px border-radius (pill değil)
- Active item gradient: Coral→Cyan
- Daha soft shadow ve glow

### 🎯 Renk Kullanımı:

| Element | Eski | Yeni |
|---------|------|------|
| Primary Button | Coral→PembeLight | Coral→Cyan |
| Feature Cards | Pembe/Cyan gradients | Coral→Cyan / Cyan→Coral |
| Badges | Amber/Pembe karışık | Sadece Cyan |
| Voice Orbs | Gradient arka plan | Solid beyaz + border |
| Nav Bar Active | Pembe gradient | Coral→Cyan |
| Live Badge | Pembe pill | Coral→Cyan, 12px radius |

### 📐 Border Radius Sistemi:

| Element | Eski | Yeni |
|---------|------|------|
| Primary Button | 9999px (pill) | 28px |
| Cards | 32-48px | 28-32px |
| Small Buttons | 9999px (pill) | 18px |
| Badges | 9999px (pill) | 20px |
| Nav Bar | 9999px (pill) | 28px |
